Pure neural weight editing does not.** A separate TRAIN-only exact certificate then proves that the fixed representation and original continuation budget cannot simultaneously copy the necessary J continuation rankings. This is a bounded policy-copy result, not a proof that every useful edit is impossible.\\n\\nThis PR stacks on #4790 at `2d0120e208ed81515837a7530c9f6943af03e87f`. Candidates, gates and four primary comparisons were frozen before their respective evaluations. Content hashes are unique against accessible history; uniqueness is not a statistical-independence guarantee. The statistical unit is the five paired fits, with df=4 and Bonferroni familywise 95% intervals.\\n\\n| Primary contrast against incumbent C | Mean TEST utility difference | Familywise 95% interval | Fixed lower-bound gate > 0.005 |\\n| --- | ---: | --- | --- |\\n| J, IID | +0.1052767633 | [0.0477670749, 0.1627864517] | PASS |\\n| J, OOD | +0.2021614690 | [0.1203594012, 0.2839635367] | PASS |\\n| Gated direct edit D, IID | 0 | [0, 0] | FAIL |\\n| Gated direct edit D, OOD | 0 | [0, 0] | FAIL |\\n\\nRaw direct and matched PGD edits reduce Q/Belief fitting MSE but reduce utility. All five candidates in each direct, PGD and random arm fail validation and restore C exactly. A, C, analytic-posterior P, full policy J, D, G, Q-only, Belief-only and random arms are retained. J uses the checkpoint's stored law and observations; evaluator truth is used only to score task value. The task is a finite risk-neutral renewal experiment, not realized trading profit or general NDU/FBSDE optimization.\\n\\nAn independent process replay reproduces all 77 frozen model/world/trajectory/summary comparisons exactly after excluding only the declared resource fields. It is the same scientific implementation and exposed worlds, not a new holdout or independent statistical replication. The initial pre-input invocation failure is retained.\\n\\n## Exact TRAIN diagnosis\\n\\nThe separately frozen diagnostic reconstructs all 144,397 original TRAIN rows and 320 J tables, matching original H/Y/W and table hashes. It extracts 21,634 zero-margin necessary continuation inequalities. For each fit, a nonnegative witness proves `d > 0` and `d² > R² ||Aᵀ y||²` in exact rational arithmetic, including a conservative binary64 error relaxation. The radius is the original ball centered at C_Q, with exact squared radius `sum(C_Q²)/4`.\\n\\nA separately written verifier reexecutes 21,458 nonzero-dual witness states across all 320 TRAIN worlds and checks the exact certificates. All five pass. No exact hidden/mask alias conflict was found. Full enumeration includes off-policy states; the result supplies no task-value loss lower bound. The five bounded dual searches all hit 3,000 iterations and report non-convergence; exact proof does not rely on solver success. Dual search uses objective gradients, but trains no neural weights and creates no new model. No new validation/TEST scoring was performed.\\n\\n## Rust implementation and migration\\n\\n- [Runtime #92](https://github.com/TrillionniumFoundation/hepta-runtime/pull/92), `e7a2688acc5504412d45160a08d2bf864be6b650`: joint trust-region head planning, exact stored-F32 update-budget verification and guarded patch compatibility.\\n- [Runtime #93](https://github.com/TrillionniumFoundation/hepta-runtime/pull/93), `990f85e46bedb8f49c0e6d0029ea6f9afbf6eac7`: bounded finite Bellman DAG planning and durable guarded recovery.\\n\\nActual MCP execution covers all 320 previously exposed TEST worlds, 246,160 nodes and 928,200 legal Q values.
